I started to drive from Lancaster to Morecambe's west end and felt really out of sorts and opened the windows to blow cold air into the car to keep me alert. It was October, so it was really cold. I was clearly drunk and took a one ton vehicle out on the road and clearly put others including myself at risk of death. It was stupid, foolish and selfish and made me feel terrible for being such an idiot. The following day I had a huge guilt trip that left me full of remorse and self hate for potentially putting others at risk of losing loved ones which I should have been more aware of. I have regretted my actions over the years and have never put myself and others at risk like this. I started to drive from Lancaster to Morecambe's west end and felt really out of sorts and opened the windows to blow cold air into the car to keep me alert. It was October, so it was really cold. I was clearly drunk and took a one ton vehicle out on the road and clearly put others including myself at risk of death. It was stupid, foolish and selfish and made me feel terrible for being such an idiot. The following day I had a huge guilt trip that left me full of remorse and self hate for potentially putting others at risk of losing loved ones which I should have been more aware of. I have regretted my actions over the years and have never put myself and others at risk like this. I had also an impending court case for a drunk and disorderly arrest which was due a few weeks later which would ultimatley be dismissed as I was arrested unlawfully and all charged were dropped. My grandma had given me a very old car as a present and I drove this up to Lancaster to my accomodation in Morecambe Bay. I felt that I was really at a low ebb and that nothing could get any worse. I was attending a saturday night party at the college and decided that I would not drink and would drive the four miles back to morecambe later that evening.   For some reason I drank alcohol and probably exceeded the legal limit however, I continued to drink and at the end of the evening decided that I was ok to drive after all, despite clearly exceeding the normal two pint safety limit.  I started to drive from Lancaster to Morecambe's west end and felt really out of sorts and opened the windows to blow cold air into the car to keep me alert. It was October, so it was really cold. I was clearly drunk and took a one ton vehicle out on the road and clearly put others including myself at risk of death. It was stupid, foolish and selfish and made me feel terrible for being such an idiot. The following day I had a huge guilt trip that left me full of remorse and self hate for potentially putting others at risk of losing loved ones which I should have been more aware of. I have regretted my actions over the years and have never put myself and others at risk like this. Having aged by 40 years I have now had time to reflect on my poor judgement made in 1979 and have come to value life and the importance of taking responsibility for my actions all those years ago</p>"}]}
